    local ls = Instance.new("Folder") ls.Name="leaderstats" ls.Parent=plr
    local c = Instance.new("IntValue") c.Name="Coins" c.Value=0 c.Parent=ls

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local DS = DSS:GetDataStore("Save_v3")

local function save(plr)
    pcall(function()
        DS:SetAsync("P"..plr.UserId, {
            Coins=plr:FindFirstChild("leaderstats") and plr.leaderstats.Coins.Value or 0,
            Level=plr:FindFirstChild("leaderstats") and plr.leaderstats.Level.Value or 1,
        })
    end)
end

local function load(plr)
    local ok,d = pcall(function() return DS:GetAsync("P"..plr.UserId) end)
    return ok and d or {Coins=0,Level=1}
end

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
    local d = load(plr)
    local ls = plr:WaitForChild("leaderstats")
    ls.Coins.Value = d.Coins or 0
    ls.Level.Value = d.Level or 1
end)

-- XELO AI // REGENERATED MAIN SERVER LOGIC
-- Rebuilds the runtime skeleton from the analysed game logic.
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local Config = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("XeloAI"):WaitForChild("GameConfig"))

local Remotes = {}
local function ensureRemote(name, class)
	local existing = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(name)
	if existing then
		Remotes[name] = existing
		return existing
	end
	local remote = Instance.new(class)
	remote.Name = name
	remote.Parent = ReplicatedStorage
	Remotes[name] = remote
	return remote
end

for name, class in pairs(Config.Remotes) do
	ensureRemote(name, class)
end

local function defaultStatValue(class)
	if class == "StringValue" then return "" end
	if class == "BoolValue" then return false end
	return 0
end

local function setupPlayer(player)
	local leaderstats = Instance.new("Folder")
	leaderstats.Name = "leaderstats"
	leaderstats.Parent = player

	for statName, class in pairs(Config.Stats) do
		local value = Instance.new(class)
		value.Name = statName
		value.Value = defaultStatValue(class)
		value.Parent = leaderstats
	end
end

]==]});table.insert(_0xD2CE,{service="ServerScriptService",name="XeloAI_RemoteHandler",class="Script",source=[==[
-- XELO AI // REGENERATED REMOTE ROUTER
-- Every analysed remote gets a validated, rate limited handler.
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")

local Config = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("XeloAI"):WaitForChild("GameConfig"))

local COOLDOWN = 0.25
local lastCall = {}

local function rateLimited(player)
	local now = os.clock()
	local previous = lastCall[player.UserId] or 0
	if now - previous < COOLDOWN then
		return true
	end
	lastCall[player.UserId] = now
	return false
end

local function sanitise(value, depth)
	depth = depth or 0
	if depth > 3 then
		return nil
	end
	local valueType = typeof(value)
	if valueType == "number" then
		return value
	elseif valueType == "string" then
		if #value > 200 then
			return string.sub(value, 1, 200)
		end
		return value
	elseif valueType == "boolean" then
		return value
	elseif valueType == "Vector3" then
		return value
	elseif valueType == "table" then
		local result = {}
		local count = 0
		for key, item in pairs(value) do
			if count >= 20 then
				break
			end
			result[key] = sanitise(item, depth + 1)
			count = count + 1
		end
		return result
	end
	return nil
end

local function makeListener(name, class)
	local remote = ReplicatedStorage:FindFirstChild(name)
	if not remote then
		remote = Instance.new(class)
		remote.Name = name
		remote.Parent = ReplicatedStorage
	end

	if not remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then
		return
	end

	remote.OnServerEvent:Connect(function(player, ...)
		if rateLimited(player) then
			return
		end
		local args = table.pack(sanitise((...)))
		print("[XeloAI] " .. name .. " called by " .. player.Name)
		-- Regenerated behaviour goes here.
		return unpack(args, 1, args.n)
	end)
end

for name, class in pairs(Config.Remotes) do
	task.spawn(makeListener, name, class)
end

print("[XeloAI] Remote router ready")
]==]});table.insert(_0xD2CE,{service="ServerScriptService",name="XeloAI_DataService",class="Script",source=[==[
-- XELO AI // REGENERATED DATA SERVICE
local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local DataStoreService = game:GetService("DataStoreService")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local ReplicatedStorage = game:GetService("ReplicatedStorage")

local Config = require(ReplicatedStorage:WaitForChild("XeloAI"):WaitForChild("GameConfig"))

local Store = DataStoreService:GetDataStore("XeloAI_v1")
local Profiles = {}

local function emptyProfile()
	local profile = {}
	for statName, class in pairs(Config.Stats) do
		profile[statName] = (class == "StringValue") and "" or (class == "BoolValue") and false or 0
	end
	return profile
end

local function loadProfile(player)
	local ok, stored = pcall(function()
		return Store:GetAsync("P" .. player.UserId)
	end)
	local profile = emptyProfile()
	if ok and type(stored) == "table" then
		for key, value in pairs(stored) do
			profile[key] = value
		end
	end
	Profiles[player.UserId] = profile
	return profile
end

local function saveProfile(player)
	local profile = Profiles[player.UserId]
	if not profile then
		return
	end
	pcall(function()
		Store:SetAsync("P" .. player.UserId, profile)
	end)
end

local function pushToLeaderstats(player)
	local profile = Profiles[player.UserId]
	local leaderstats = player:FindFirstChild("leaderstats")
	if not profile or not leaderstats then
		return
	end
	for statName, value in pairs(profile) do
		local object = leaderstats:FindFirstChild(statName)
		if object and object:IsA("ValueBase") then
			object.Value = value
		end
	end
end

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
	loadProfile(player)
	player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
		task.wait(1)
		pushToLeaderstats(player)
	end)
	task.defer(pushToLeaderstats, player)
end)

Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(player)
	saveProfile(player)
	Profiles[player.UserId] = nil
end)

game:BindToClose(function()
	for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		saveProfile(player)
	end
end)

task.spawn(function()
	while task.wait(120) do
		for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
			task.spawn(saveProfile, player)
		end
	end
end)

print("[XeloAI] Data service ready")

]==]});table.insert(_0xD2CE,{service="ServerScriptService",name="XeloAI_AntiCheat",class="Script",source=[==[
-- XELO AI // REGENERATED SERVER ANTI-CHEAT
-- Continuously clamps speed/jump and normalises impossible health values.
-- Conservative by design: it never kicks on false positives, it only
-- neutralises invalid state, so re-gameplay stays safe for real players.
local Players = game:GetService("Players")

local CONFIG = {
	MaxWalkSpeed = 100, -- anything above is reverted to the default speed
	MaxJumpPower = 200,
	CheckInterval = 1,
}

local function guardHumanoid(player, character)
	local humanoid = character:WaitForChild("Humanoid", 10)
	if not humanoid then return end

	task.spawn(function()
		while character.Parent and humanoid.Parent do
			if humanoid.WalkSpeed > CONFIG.MaxWalkSpeed then
				humanoid.WalkSpeed = 16
			end
			if humanoid.UseJumpPower and humanoid.JumpPower > CONFIG.MaxJumpPower then
				humanoid.JumpPower = 50
			end
			task.wait(CONFIG.CheckInterval)
		end
	end)

	humanoid.HealthChanged:Connect(function(health)
		-- NaN, negative and above-max writes are impossible server-side and get
		-- normalised instead of corrupting the player's state.
		if health ~= health or health < 0 then
			humanoid.Health = math.max(0, humanoid.Health)
		end
	end)
end

Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(player)
	player.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(character)
		task.spawn(guardHumanoid, player, character)
	end)
	if player.Character then
		task.spawn(guardHumanoid, player, player.Character)
	end
end)

print("[XeloAI] Anti-cheat ready")

local function apply(target, data)
    for key, value in pairs(data.properties or {}) do
        pcall(function() target[key] = value end)
    end
    return target
end

function VFX.apply(target, data)
    return apply(target, data)
end

function VFX.list()
    local names = {}
    for name in pairs(VFX.__effects) do
        names[#names + 1] = name
    end
    table.sort(names)
    return names
end

function VFX.get(name)
    return VFX.__effects[name]
end

function VFX.build(name, parent)
    local data = VFX.__effects[name]
    if not data then
        warn("[Xelo VFX] Unknown effect: " .. tostring(name))
        return nil
    end
    local ok, instance = pcall(Instance.new, data.class)
    if not ok or not instance then
        warn("[Xelo VFX] Cannot create class " .. tostring(data.class))
        return nil
    end
    apply(instance, data)
    instance.Name = name
    instance.Parent = parent or workspace
    return instance
end

function VFX.buildAll(parent)
    local created = {}
    for _, name in ipairs(VFX.list()) do
        local instance = VFX.build(name, parent)
        if instance then
            created[#created + 1] = instance
        end
    end
    return created
end
